# Parity inventory — method, field ids and per-page mapping

> Generated by `parity/inventory/tools/gen_parity_doc.py` from `parity/inventory/*.yaml`. Do not hand-edit: edit the YAML (or this script for prose) and regenerate.

This is the **P0 ground truth** of PLAN.md §8: for every field a stock YARN UI shows, the label, its canonical field id (`docs/contracts/parity-dom.md`), where the value comes from in the RM/NM/JHS REST API (or why it has no REST source: `derived`), the display format and the normaliser the differential harness needs.

Everything here is derived from the **Hadoop 3.4.3 sources** (`.cluster/src-extract/`, extracted by `parity/inventory/tools/extract-sources.sh`) plus the **compiled UI2 bundle** in `.cluster/hadoop/share/hadoop/yarn/webapps/ui2` (split/decoded by `tools/ui2_extract.py` + `tools/ui2_decode_templates.js`). Nothing is quoted from memory; every item carries a `source: file:line` pointer and every REST object/field a DAO source line.

## 1. How to reproduce

```sh
# 1. sources (idempotent; needs the Hadoop tree from cluster provisioning)
parity/inventory/tools/extract-sources.sh
# 2. per-module REST/legacy extracts (each writes one src/*.yaml file)
.venv/bin/python parity/inventory/tools/gen_rest_rm.py        # src/rest-rm.yaml  (RM REST objects+resources)
.venv/bin/python parity/inventory/tools/gen_rest_nm.py        # src/rest-nm.yaml  (NM REST objects+resources)
bun parity/inventory/tools/ui2_extract.py                      # ui2 bundle -> .cluster/src-extract/ui2
bun parity/inventory/tools/ui2_decode_templates.js             # compiled HTMLBars -> readable .hbs.txt
# 3. merge + registry + validation (all offline)
.venv/bin/python parity/inventory/build.py                     # rest-schema.yaml, legacy-pages.yaml, field-ids.yaml
.venv/bin/python parity/inventory/validate.py                  # JSON Schema + referential integrity + totals
.venv/bin/python parity/inventory/tools/gen_parity_doc.py --write   # this document

# 4. optional live shape check against the running cluster (P1-lite: every registered REST id path must resolve)
.venv/bin/python parity/inventory/tools/live_probe.py
```

`build.py` is idempotent; `validate.py` exits 1 on any failure and is the gate for this slice. The per-page extracts under `src/` are hand-curated (labels verbatim from the Java, `source:` pointers) but their *shapes* are machine-checked: `validate.py` proves every `field:` resolves to a registry row and every REST JSON path reachable from a GET resource is either registered or explicitly waived.

## 2. Totals

| what | count |
|---|---|
| inventory pages | 74 (22 legacy, 52 UI2) |
| sections | 179 |
| items (labels/columns/links/charts) | 1143 (544 legacy, 599 UI2) |
| REST entities | 33 |
| REST objects (DAO shapes) | 126 |
| REST object fields (Java members serialised) | 655 |
| REST resources (endpoints) | 99 |
| derived (non-REST) declarations | 158 |
| field-id registry rows | 1574 (rest 1055, derived 158, static 361) |
| registry: required (a legacy/UI2 item renders it) | 798 |
| registry: waivable (with a reason) | 776 |
| registry shownBy legacy / ui2 / both / rest-only | 376 / 319 / 103 / 776 |
| REST JSON paths waived without a field id | 7 |

Waiver categories (each row in `field-ids.yaml` carries a `waiver.reason`; there are no silent skips):

| category | rows |
|---|---|
| rendered-elsewhere | 504 |
| unused-by-any-ui | 261 |
| other | 9 |
| write-only | 1 |
| internal | 1 |

`rest-only` rows are keys the stock UIs do **not** render (they are still catalogued so PLAN §8 P1 can prove the SPA does not miss them); `composite` rows are whole `Resource` objects that YARN prints as one formatted string, e.g. `queue.resourcesUsed` -> `"<memory:1024, vCores:2>"`.

## 3. Method

### 3.1 Where labels come from

* **Legacy Hamlet UI**: the Java `*Block`/`*Page`/`*Table` classes under `hadoop-yarn-server-resourcemanager/.../webapp/`, `hadoop-yarn-server-common/.../webapp/` and `hadoop-yarn-common/.../webapp/view/`. Labels are the verbatim string literals of the Hamlet calls (`.th(".id", "Attempt ID")`, `.h3("Scheduler Health")`, `info(..).__("Queue State:", …)`); DataTables column class names (`<th class=".allocatedMB">`) are recorded in `note:` because the legacy table feeds the DOM *class* to the renderer (`yarn.dt.plugins.js` renderHadoopDate/parseHadoopProgress).

* **UI2 (Ember)**: the compiled bundle is split into modules and its precompiled HTMLBars templates are decoded back to pseudo-Handlebars, so literal labels are exact; column definitions come from `yarn-ui/controllers/*-table-columns.js` / component `columns` arrays, REST paths from `yarn-ui/adapters/*.js`, and attribute names from `yarn-ui/models/*.js`.

### 3.2 Field ids

`<entity>.<JSON key path relative to the entity object>` with array indices dropped (see `docs/contracts/parity-dom.md`). Entities are declared once, in a REST part (`src/rest-*.yaml`), because the id must be derivable from the upstream JSON. Values the stock UI computes instead of printing a REST key get `<entity>.derived.<camelName>` declared once with the Java/JS expression that produces them. Pure chrome gets `static.<name>`.

### 3.3 REST shapes (JAXB/Jersey) — the parts that bite

Hadoop 3.4.3 serialises RM/NM/AHS/JHS DAOs through Jersey 1 `JSONJAXBContext`. Three behaviours are live-verified against the running cluster (2026-10-03) and are recorded per object as `jsonMode`:

| mode | who | behaviour |
|---|---|---|
| `wrapped` | classes in `JAXBContextResolver` `cTypes` | body is `{"<xmlRoot>":{…}}`, native JSON types (`{"apps":{"app":[…]}}`, `"progress":100.0`) |
| `unwrapped` | classes in `rootUnwrappedTypes` | root element dropped, native types (`{"priority":0}`) |
| `unwrapped-stringified` | every other class (Jersey default provider) | root dropped **and every scalar stringified** (`{"allocatedMB":"768"}`, `"exclusivity":"false"`) |
| `nested` | objects only used inside another object | inherits the parent context |

Other quirks that the harness must normalise (each also noted on the field):

* **null properties are omitted**, not emitted as `null` (e.g. `ClusterInfo.subClusterId`, `NodeInfo.healthReport` is `""` though).

* **`-1` sentinels**: `AppInfo.allocatedMB/allocatedVCores/reservedMB/reservedVCores/runningContainers` are `-1` for finished applications; `AppTimeoutInfo.remainingTimeInSeconds` is `-1` with `expiryTime:"UNLIMITED"` when unset.

* **single-element collections collapse** to a bare element in the fallback/stringified contexts (`/nodes/x/get-labels` with one label) and for JAXB map adapters with one entry (`AppInfo.resourceSecondsMap` -> `{"entry":{"key":..,"value":..}}`). Arrays of DAO objects in wrapped contexts stay arrays even with one element (live-verified with a 1-application `/apps?states=FAILED`).

* **maps** are serialised through the JAXB map adapter as `{"entry":[{"key":…,"value":…}, …]}`.

* **empty results omit the key**: `/apps` with no match is `{"apps":{}}`, `/…/containers` is `{}`.

* **`type` discriminators**: `capacityScheduler` / `fairScheduler` / `fifoScheduler` / `capacitySchedulerLeafQueueInfo` … are injected by the JSON provider from `@XmlType`/the class name, not Java fields (registry rows marked `synthetic`).

* `SchedulerInfo` is an abstract base whose protected fields are **not** serialised under the subclasses' `@XmlAccessorType(FIELD)`; only the discriminator appears.

### 3.4 Conditional pages and items

`when:` uses the vocabulary in FORMAT.md (`scheduler=capacity|fair|fifo`, `state=…`, `conf:x=y`, `labels`, `ha`, `secure`, `value:null|-1|non-empty`, `ui:<flag>`, `other:…`). Items that only appear with a particular cluster state also carry `scenario:` (what must exist on the test cluster to see them). Every such item is listed in §6.

## 4. Per-page mapping (label -> field id -> REST source -> format)

`REST` is `<resource id> @ <JSON path from the response body>`; empty means the value is derived (the `derivation` column shows the Java/JS expression) or static chrome. `fmt`/`norm` are the display format and the normaliser the differential harness applies to the legacy text.

### `legacy.rm.apps` — {app.state\|All} Applications

* file: `parity/inventory/legacy-pages.yaml`; daemon: `rm`; route: `/cluster \| /cluster/apps \| /cluster/apps/{app.state} \| /cluster/queue/{queue.name} \| /cluster/failure(no)`
* classes: `RmController#index`, `RmController#queue`, `RmView`, `AppsBlockWithMetrics`, `MetricsOverviewTable`, `RMAppsBlock`, `AppsBlock`, `ColumnHeader`, `WebPageUtils`, `NavBlock`
* includes: `legacy.rm.shared.chrome`, `legacy.rm.shared.metrics`
* query params: `app.state` — path param (RMWebApp: pajoin("/apps", APP_STATE)); comma separated YarnApplicationState names; empty => All; AppsBlock.fetchData splits on ',' and YarnApplicationState.valueOf(trim), `apps.num` — limit of applications returned (AppsBlock.fetchData -> request.setLimit), `app.started-time.begin` — epoch ms lower bound (must be >=0), `app.started-time.end` — epoch ms upper bound (must be >=begin), `queue.name` — route /cluster/queue/{queue.name} only changes the TITLE to 'Queue <name>' (RmController.queue); it does NOT filter the table

### `legacy.rm.errors` — Errors and Warnings in the ResourceManager

* file: `parity/inventory/legacy-pages.yaml`; daemon: `rm`; route: `/cluster/errors-and-warnings`

### `legacy.rm.nodes` — Nodes of the cluster

* file: `parity/inventory/legacy-pages.yaml`; daemon: `rm`; route: `/cluster/nodes \| /cluster/nodes/{node.state}`
* classes: `RmController#nodes`, `NodesPage`, `NodesBlock`, `MetricsOverviewTable`, `NavBlock`, `WebPageUtils`
* includes: `legacy.rm.shared.chrome`, `legacy.rm.shared.metrics`
* query params: `node.state` — route path param (RMWebApp.java:68 pajoin("/nodes", NODE_STATE); YarnWebParams.NODE_STATE = "node.state"); a NodeState enum name, matched case-insensitively (NodesPage.java:128-129 NodeState.valueOf(StringUtils.toUpperCase(type))); filters the table and appends " (<type>)" to the title (NodesPage.java:272-276); empty => all active RM nodes except UNHEALTHY (NodesPage.java:153-162); DECOMMISSIONED\|LOST\|REBOOTED\|SHUTDOWN read getInactiveRMNodes (NodesPage.java:131-144), `node.label` — request param (NodesPage.java:75 $(NODE_LABEL, CommonNodeLabelsManager.ANY).trim(); YarnWebParams.NODE_LABEL = "node.label"); CommonNodeLabelsManager.ANY = "*" disables label filtering; empty string shows only nodes with no label; otherwise only nodes whose nodeLabels contains that label (NodesPage.java:165-180). Targets of the Node Labels page active-NM links /cluster/nodes?node.label=<label>

### `legacy.rm.scheduler.fair` — Fair Scheduler

* file: `parity/inventory/legacy-pages.yaml`; daemon: `rm`; route: `/cluster/scheduler`
* classes: `RmController#scheduler`, `FairSchedulerPage`, `FSQInfo`, `LeafQueueBlock`, `ParentQueueBlock`, `QueueBlock`, `QueuesBlock`, `FairSchedulerAppsBlock`, `SchedulerPageUtil.QueueBlockUtil`, `MetricsOverviewTable`
* includes: `legacy.rm.shared.chrome`, `legacy.rm.shared.metrics`
* available when: `scheduler=fair`
* query params: `openQueues` — queue names separated by '#' auto-expanded in the queue tree; managed by SchedulerPageUtil (FairSchedulerPage.postHead includes QueueBlockUtil)

## 5. REST resource coverage

Every RM/NM/JHS/AHS/daemon resource a legacy or UI2 page uses is declared, including the ones **outside** the `docs/contracts/api.md` allowlist (`allowlist: false`) so nothing is invisible. Write endpoints are listed with `readOnly: false` and excluded from the product.

| resource | daemon | method | path | in contract | read-only | response root |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ats.flows` | common | GET | `/ws/v2/timeline/flows` | no | yes | ``  |
| `ats.flow-runs` | common | GET | `/ws/v2/timeline/flow-uid/{flowuid}/runs` | no | yes | ``  |
| `ats.flow-run` | common | GET | `/ws/v2/timeline/run-uid/{flowrunuid}` | no | yes | ``  |
| `ats.flow-run-apps` | common | GET | `/ws/v2/timeline/run-uid/{flowrunuid}/apps` | no | yes | ``  |

| `ats.entities` | common | GET | `/ws/v2/timeline/apps/{appId}/entities/{entityType}` | no | yes | ``  |
| `ats.health` | common | GET | `/ws/v2/timeline/health` | no | yes | ``  |
| `ats.log` | common | GET | `/ws/v2/applicationlog/containers/{containerId}/logs/{file}` | no | yes | ``  |

## 7. Values with no REST source (`restGap`)

These are shown by YARN but not exposed by any contracted endpoint. Where the information is obtainable elsewhere the `altSource` says how (usually `/jmx`); otherwise the SPA cannot reproduce the exact value and the harness must waive it explicitly.

| derived id | entity | derivation | alt source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `queue.derived.freeGuaranteedPct` | queue | `absoluteCapacity - absoluteUsedCapacity, clamped at >= 0 (root queue: capacity - usedCapacity)` | _none_ |
| `queue.derived.burstRoomPct` | queue | `absoluteMaxCapacity - absoluteUsedCapacity, clamped at >= 0 (root queue: maxCapacity - usedCapacity)` | _none_ |
| `queue.derived.fairShareFreeMb` | queue | `fairResources.memory - usedResources.memory` | _none_ |
| `queue.derived.usedOfFairPct` | queue | `usedResources.memory / fairResources.memory * 100` | _none_ |
| `queue.derived.usedOfMinPct` | queue | `usedResources.memory / minResources.memory * 100` | _none_ |
| `queue.derived.headroomMb` | queue | `capacity: maxEffectiveCapacity.memory - resourcesUsed.memory ; fair: maxResources.memory - usedResources.memory` | _none_ |
| `attempt.derived.resourcePreemptedText` | attempt | `attemptMetrics == null ? Resources.none() : attemptMetrics.getResourcePreempted()  (Resource.toString)` | none |
| `attempt.derived.numNonAMContainersPreempted` | attempt | `attemptMetrics == null ? 0 : attemptMetrics.getNumNonAMContainersPreempted()` | none |
| `attempt.derived.appBlacklistedNodesCount` | attempt | `rmAppAttempt.getBlacklistedNodes().size()` | count of ', '-separated ids in attempt.blacklistedNodes (rm.app.attempts) |
| `attempt.derived.rmBlacklistedNodesCount` | attempt | `rmAppAttempt.getAMBlacklistManager().getBlacklistUpdates().getBlacklistAdditions().size()` | count of ', '-separated ids in attempt.nodesBlacklistedBySystem (rm.app.attempts) |
| `attempt.derived.appBlacklistedNodesText` | attempt | `getNodeString(rmAppAttempt.getBlacklistedNodes()) => StringUtils.join(nodes, ", ") or "-" when null/empty` | attempt.blacklistedNodes (rm.app.attempts, ', '-joined) |
| `attempt.derived.rmBlacklistedNodesText` | attempt | `getNodeString(rmAppAttempt.getAMBlacklistManager().getBlacklistUpdates().getBlacklistAdditions()) => join ", " or "-"` | attempt.nodesBlacklistedBySystem (rm.app.attempts) |
| `attempt.derived.headroomText` | attempt | `metrics == null ? "N/A" : metrics.getApplicationAttemptHeadroom()` | none |
| `attempt.derived.totalAllocatedContainersText` | attempt | `"Total Allocated Containers: " + attemptMetrics.getTotalAllocatedContainers()` | none |
| `attempt.derived.localityStatistics` | attempt | `attemptMetrics.getLocalityStatistics()[i][j]  (rows i=0 NodeLocal,1 RackLocal,2 OffSwitch; cols j=0..2)` | none |
| `attempt.derived.totalOutstandingResourceRequestsText` | attempt | `"Total Outstanding Resource Requests: " + getTotalResource(resourceRequests)  (sum over requests with resourceName==ANY of capability * numContainers)` | compute from app.resourceRequests[] |
| `app.derived.allocatedGpus` | app | `(isAppInCompletedState && app.getAllocatedGpus() <= 0) ? UNAVAILABLE : String.valueOf(app.getAllocatedGpus());  allocatedGpus = ApplicationResourceUsageReport.g` | none for current usage in /apps/{id}; app.resourceSecondsMap has cumulative gpu-seconds only; app.resourceInfo.resourceUsagesByPartition[].used.resourceInformations (Capacity only) [INFERENCE - verify live] |
| `app.derived.reservedGpus` | app | `(isAppInCompletedState && app.getReservedGpus() <= 0) ? UNAVAILABLE : String.valueOf(app.getReservedGpus())` | app.resourceInfo.resourceUsagesByPartition[].reserved.resourceInformations (Capacity only) [INFERENCE - verify live] |
| `app.derived.blacklistedNodesCount` | app | `appAttempt = rmApp.getRMAppAttempt(ApplicationAttemptId.fromString(app.getCurrentAppAttemptId())); nodes = appAttempt.getBlacklistedNodes(); N/A if null else St` | count of comma-separated ids in attempt.blacklistedNodes of the current attempt (rm.app.attempts) [INFERENCE: REST joins sattempt.getBlacklistedNodes() with ', ' only if hasAccess and scheduler attempt exists] |
| `cluster.metrics.derived.log4jErrorCount1Min` | cluster.metrics | `appender.getErrorCounts(getCutoffs()).get(0)  (index i = i-th cutoff; 1 min window)` | none |
| `cluster.metrics.derived.log4jErrorCount5Min` | cluster.metrics | `appender.getErrorCounts(getCutoffs()).get(1)` | none |
| `cluster.metrics.derived.log4jErrorCount15Min` | cluster.metrics | `appender.getErrorCounts(getCutoffs()).get(2)` | none |
| `cluster.metrics.derived.log4jErrorCount1Hour` | cluster.metrics | `appender.getErrorCounts(getCutoffs()).get(3)` | none |
| `cluster.metrics.derived.log4jErrorCount6Hours` | cluster.metrics | `appender.getErrorCounts(getCutoffs()).get(4)` | none |
| `cluster.metrics.derived.log4jErrorCount12Hours` | cluster.metrics | `appender.getErrorCounts(getCutoffs()).get(5)` | none |
| `cluster.metrics.derived.log4jErrorCount24Hours` | cluster.metrics | `appender.getErrorCounts(getCutoffs()).get(6)  (NOTE: cutoff is now-84600s, source typo for 86400)` | none |
| `cluster.metrics.derived.log4jWarningCount1Min` | cluster.metrics | `appender.getWarningCounts(getCutoffs()).get(0)` | none |
| `cluster.metrics.derived.log4jWarningCount5Min` | cluster.metrics | `appender.getWarningCounts(getCutoffs()).get(1)` | none |
| `cluster.metrics.derived.log4jWarningCount15Min` | cluster.metrics | `appender.getWarningCounts(getCutoffs()).get(2)` | none |
| `cluster.metrics.derived.log4jWarningCount1Hour` | cluster.metrics | `appender.getWarningCounts(getCutoffs()).get(3)` | none |
| `cluster.metrics.derived.log4jWarningCount6Hours` | cluster.metrics | `appender.getWarningCounts(getCutoffs()).get(4)` | none |
| `cluster.metrics.derived.log4jWarningCount12Hours` | cluster.metrics | `appender.getWarningCounts(getCutoffs()).get(5)` | none |

## 8. Honest "needs live verification" list

Everything below is source-derived and structurally verified, but a claim in it has **not** been confirmed against a live daemon. `[INFERENCE]` appears in the corresponding `note`. PLAN §8 P1/P2 must confirm each one before it is treated as ground truth.

| page | item | why |
|---|---|---|
| legacy.rm.apps | `legacy.rm.apps.page.title` | <title>/h1 = sjoin(reqState-or-'All', 'Applications') e.g. 'RUNNING Applications'. AppsBlock.render() later calls setTitle('Applications') but after the header was rendered, so it is NOT visible [INFERENCE - verify live]. /cluster/queue/{q} sets 'Queue <q>' in the controller but RmView.preHead overw |
| legacy.rm.shared.chrome | `legacy.rm.shared.chrome.header.user` | REST requestedUser is UGI user of the caller; legacy uses servlet getRemoteUser(). Default unauthenticated RM shows dr.who (StaticUserWebFilter) [INFERENCE - verify live] |
| - | `app.derived.allocatedGpus` | none for current usage in /apps/{id}; app.resourceSecondsMap has cumulative gpu-seconds only; app.resourceInfo.resourceUsagesByPartition[].used.resourceInformations (Capacity only) [INFERENCE - verify live] |
| - | `app.derived.reservedGpus` | app.resourceInfo.resourceUsagesByPartition[].reserved.resourceInformations (Capacity only) [INFERENCE - verify live] |
| - | `app.derived.blacklistedNodesCount` | count of comma-separated ids in attempt.blacklistedNodes of the current attempt (rm.app.attempts) [INFERENCE: REST joins sattempt.getBlacklistedNodes() with ', ' only if hasAccess and scheduler attempt exists] |
| - | `scheduler.derived.aggregateAllocatedContainers` | none in REST [INFERENCE - QueueMetrics JMX may expose it] |
| - | `usermetrics.derived.appsSubmitted` | /jmx bean Hadoop:service=ResourceManager,name=QueueMetrics,q0=root,user=<user> attribute AppsSubmitted [INFERENCE - verify live] |
| - | `scheduler.derived.maximumClusterPriority` | none in REST [INFERENCE - confirm with rest-schema scheduler fields] |

Known environmental facts that must be re-checked per YARN version (all verified on 3.4.3 today):

* `rm.resource-profiles`, `rm.resource-types`, `rm.containers` are in the api.md allowlist but are **not registered by this build** (live 404) — see the contract change requests.

* The `/conf` content negotiation is **`Accept`-driven**: `?format=json` is ignored (live-verified), so the backend must send `Accept: application/json` for `{"properties":[…]}` and the API contract line mentioning `?format=json` is wrong.

* `apps/{appId}/appactivities` in api.md §3 does not exist; the real path is `/scheduler/app-activities/{appid}`.

Shape check performed on 2026-10-03 with `tools/live_probe.py` against the live RM (capacity scheduler, 3 NMs, ~30 applications): 643 registered RM id paths resolved, 331 were absent for the then-current cluster state (optional/null fields such as `AppInfo.appNodeLabelExpression`, `PartitionResourcesInfo.amLimit`, `ResourceInformation.tags`, or scheduler activities that need `yarn.resourcemanager.scheduler.activities.enable`), 0 wrong paths; the NM/JHS/AHS ids are probed with `--all`.

## 9. Contract change requests (for the integration owner)

The inventory is the authority for the field ids, so these are the places where `docs/contracts/api.md` and `docs/contracts/parity-dom.md` disagree with the real 3.4.3 daemons:

1. **api.md §3**: add `scheduler-overview` (it is the 1:1 source of the legacy scheduler info block and is already rendered by the SPA), `userinfo`, `apps/{appId}/appattempts/{attemptId}` and `nodes/{nodeId}/get-labels`. Remove or mark unsupported `resource-profiles`, `resource-types`, `containers/{containerId}` (all 404 on 3.4.3).

2. **api.md §3**: replace `apps/{appId}/appactivities` with `scheduler/app-activities/{appId}`.

3. **api.md §3 daemon pages**: `/conf` is `Accept`-driven (no `?format=json`); `/metrics` is not registered by the RM in 3.4.3 (404) and must be dropped or feature-gated.

4. **parity-dom.md §Field-id grammar**: the entity list needs the entities this inventory actually declares (listed in `rest-schema.yaml`), notably `clusteruser`, `usermetrics`, `queueuser`, `activities`, `appactivity`, `timeout`, `queueacl`, `jmx`, `log`, `conf`, the `nm.*` family and the UI2-only `ats*` entities; and the `data-yv` rule should say that stringified upstream scalars keep the upstream spelling (`"768"`).

5. **PLAN §8 P1**: the waivers for REST keys the stock UI does not render belong in `parity/waivers.yaml`; `field-ids.yaml` (`status: waivable` + `waiver.reason`) is the input list for it.

```
33 entities, 126 objects (655 object fields), 99 resources
74 pages, 179 sections, 1143 items
registry: 1574 rows (rest 1055, derived 158, static 361); required 798, waivable 776
REST paths waived without a field id: 7
```
